current_state: The source tree now defaults normal `/generate` image generation to Gemini. The frontend uses `VITE_GENERATION_PROVIDER=gemini`, shows `Geminiで生成`, and sends `generationProvider: gemini`; the production `generate-image` Edge Function has been redeployed with the Gemini path and defaults to Gemini, enforces auth, brand editor role, feature allowlist, input validation, legal-safety rights confirmation, quota/rate limits, then calls server-side `_shared/geminiImage.ts`. Gemini requires `GEMINI_API_KEY` only in server-side runtime secrets and fails closed as `gemini_api_key_missing` / `gemini_image_request_failed` when the key is absent or invalid. Runway MCP/local-worker remains an explicit fallback/legacy path for `generationProvider=runway` or `VITE_GENERATION_PROVIDER=local-runway`, with the existing approved-client handoff/import worker retained for historical proofs and deliberate Runway recovery. H602 billing-readiness groundwork restores monthly quota enforcement and keeps Apple sandbox/tester no-real-charge verification as human/operator-gated work; migration `20260701143000_roll_free_subscription_period_for_usage_reservation.sql` is applied to production so active free-plan brands roll across month boundaries without enabling checkout or payment. Live Gemini production generation now reaches Google Generative Language with the active server-side secret and default Nano Banana model `gemini-2.5-flash-image`, but is blocked exactly by Gemini image-generation free-tier quota `limit: 0`, not by Runway, UI wiring, quota period, legal-safety false positive, missing/invalid key, API service enablement, or wrong model name.

Verification passed: `node --check scripts/create-g619-beta-session.mjs`, `node --check scripts/verify-g619-beta-evidence.mjs`, scaffold generation at `output/playwright/g619-real-beta-evidence-g626-scaffold-check/manifest.json`, fail-closed verifier proofs `output/playwright/g619-real-beta-evidence-g626-scaffold-check/summary.json` / `output/playwright/g619-real-beta-evidence-g626-scaffold-check/summary-rerun.json` (`ok=false` as expected because real consent, real duration, desktop/mobile/persona coverage, behavior evidence, friction notes, and full workflow coverage are still missing), and init-template fail-closed proof `output/playwright/g619-real-beta-evidence-g626-init-template-check/summary.json`. Refreshed incomplete 10M audit `output/playwright/10m-completion-audit-20260701-g626-r2/summary.json` remains `ok=false` only for the known G617/G619/H601/H602/public-domain/dependent-gate blockers. G619 remains queued/human-needed until real consented sessions pass `npm run verify:g619-beta-evidence`.

lab_workspace_generation_flow_20260701: G656 makes `/lab` usable as a production flow and fixes the hidden evaluation preview gap. `LabPage` now shows a top Lab flow panel with hypothesis, evaluation, and output readiness plus direct actions to campaign-image generation, Canvas save, and Gallery review. The page now renders the deterministic Lab evaluation SVG and QA requires `lab-evaluation-local-v1`, `selected-lab-experiment`, `scoreSignature`, and `Next step`, so it cannot regress into score-only cards or a hidden/generic preview without failing mass-market QA. Local proof `output/playwright/local-g656-lab-flow-20260701-r1/SUMMARY.json` passed with `ok=true`, `failed=[]` and screenshot inspection; production proof after push `e5ad209` passed at `output/playwright/prod-post-g656-lab-flow-20260701-r1/SUMMARY.json` with 17 desktop routes, 9 mobile routes, zero console/page/request failures, and both Lab assertions. Production monitor `output/playwright/production-monitor-post-g656-20260701-r1/summary.json` passed with `ok=true`, blockers 0, and `uiOk=true`. This improves Lab usability but does not close G617/G619/H601/H602 or the custom domain blocker.
marketing_workspace_generation_flow_20260701: G657 makes `/marketing` usable as a production flow and fixes the weak brief-preview gate. `MarketingWorkspacePage` now shows a top Marketing flow panel with material, appeal, and output readiness plus direct actions to campaign-image generation, Canvas save, and Gallery review. It renders a deterministic Marketing brief SVG and QA requires `marketing-brief-local-v1`, `selected-marketing-brief`, `selectedTemplate`, and `Next step`, so the page cannot regress to a text-only or hidden/generic preview without failing mass-market QA. `workspaceHandoff` now includes `marketing` as an allowed source for generation-intent links. Local proof `output/playwright/local-g657-marketing-flow-20260701-r1/SUMMARY.json` passed with `ok=true`, `failed=[]` and screenshot inspection; production proof after push `72476bc` passed on rerun at `output/playwright/prod-post-g657-marketing-flow-20260701-r2/SUMMARY.json` with 17 desktop routes, 9 mobile routes, zero console/page/request failures, and both Marketing assertions. Production QA r1 passed route assertions but is not adopted because browser cleanup timed out. Production monitor `output/playwright/production-monitor-post-g657-20260701-r1/summary.json` passed with `ok=true`, blockers 0, and `uiOk=true`. This improves Marketing usability but does not close G617/G619/H601/H602 or the custom domain blocker.
fitting_workspace_generation_flow_20260701: G658 makes `/fitting` usable as a production flow and fixes the meaningless preview gap. `FittingPage` now shows a top Fitting flow panel with selected body/age/gender/readiness context, direct model-matrix generation handoff, AI generation action state, and Gallery review. The page renders a deterministic fitting brief SVG that contains `fitting-brief-local-v1`, `selected-fitting-workflow`, pattern count, body types, age groups, gender, material, layer, placement, and `Next step`, so the preview can no longer be a generic decorative image without failing mass-market QA. `workspaceHandoff` now includes `fitting` as an allowed source for generation-intent links. Local proof `output/playwright/local-g658-fitting-flow-20260701-r1/SUMMARY.json` passed with `ok=true`, `failed=[]` and screenshot inspection; production proof after push `724aae6` passed at `output/playwright/prod-post-g658-fitting-flow-20260701-r1/SUMMARY.json` with 17 desktop routes, 9 mobile routes, zero console/page/request failures, and both Fitting assertions. Production monitor `output/playwright/production-monitor-post-g658-20260701-r1/summary.json` passed with `ok=true`, blockers 0, and `uiOk=true`. This improves Fitting usability but does not close G617/G619/H601/H602 or the custom domain blocker.
lightchain_order_preview_readback_20260701: G659 replaces the fixed generic Lightchain workspace artifact preview (`Lightchain compatible brief / Heavy Chain Canvas order sheet`) with a tool-specific `lightchain-order-sheet-v1` SVG. The preview now records selected tool id/route, request, reference note, material/layer/placement/scale, selected mask candidate, output targets, and next step, while the Canvas still stores the actual uploaded material, extracted cutout, and overlay layers. `saveWorkspaceArtifactBestEffort` now bounds remote `marketing-workspace-artifact` saves to 8s and falls back to localStorage with `remoteSaveStage=timeout`, so slow production remote artifact persistence no longer blocks the user-visible Canvas save flow. Local proof `output/playwright/local-g659-lightchain-order-preview-20260701-r1/SUMMARY.json` passed before the timeout fallback fix with `ok=true`, 33 features, 587 assertions, 33 order-sheet preview assertions, zero console/page/request failures, and cleanup closed; production proof after pushes `5dac2bd` and `d18cc51` passed at `output/playwright/prod-post-g659-lightchain-order-preview-20260701-r2/SUMMARY.json` with the same 33/33 tool-specific order-sheet preview assertions and cleanup closed. Production monitor `output/playwright/production-monitor-post-g659-20260701-r1/summary.json` passed with `ok=true`, blockers 0, and `uiOk=true`. This improves Lightchain/Gallery/History artifact usability but does not close G617/G619/H601/H602 or the custom domain blocker.
dashboard_lightchain_direct_cards_20260701: G660 removes the Dashboard embedded Lightchain side-detail interaction that made feature cards feel like they only opened a side explanation. Dashboard Lightchain feature cards are now direct links to `/lightchain/:toolId` dedicated screens, with the compact mobile hub still preserving the all-tools `/lightchain` link. Local proof `output/playwright/local-g660-dashboard-lightchain-direct-links-20260701-r2/SUMMARY.json` passed with `ok=true`, `failed=[]`, zero console/page/request failures, and screenshot inspection of the full-width card grid without the old right-side detail panel. Production proof after push `5f6b282` passed at `output/playwright/prod-post-g660-dashboard-lightchain-direct-links-20260701-r1/SUMMARY.json` with 17 desktop routes, 9 mobile routes, zero console/page/request failures, cleanup closed, and `mobile_dashboard_lightchain_cards_open_detail_routes`. Production monitor rerun `output/playwright/production-monitor-post-g660-20260701-r2/summary.json` passed with `ok=true`, blockers 0, and `uiOk=true` after the launch-ops mobile route wait was made robust against transient loading. Release and completion gates now require the G660 direct-detail-route assertion. This improves Dashboard-to-Lightchain usability but does not close G617/G619/H601/H602 or the custom domain blocker.
next_action: Keep the NisenPrints-proven Runway path as the production operator flow: start `npm run worker:local-runway:watch`, generate through an approved existing Runway MCP client, save the resulting MCP JSON into `output/runway-mcp-results/inbox` with `heavyChainJobId` or `generationJobId`, and let watch mode move successful files to `processed/`. Single-job `--mcp-result` imports may leave the source JSON in place as an audit artifact. Untagged JSON is not consumed by default; only use `--allow-unmatched-mcp-result` for a deliberate one-off recovery. Do not use `localhost:15554` consent pages; that is the `mcp-remote` dynamic-client path and currently fails Runway consent. `--live-runway` remains diagnostic only, and direct Runway API or hosted bridge should not be used as a fallback.
blocker: No active production blocker remains for verified real-image generation path, 2026-06-26 Lightchain product-excellence UI slice, 2026-06-26 generation-polish slice, 2026-06-26 production monitoring loop, `lightchain-full-clone-workbench-and-all-feature-qa-20260626` UI shell, G618 scale/ops baseline, G620 security-operations baseline, G619 beta evidence acceptance gate, 2026-07-01 Lightchain beginner detail-route UX slice, Zeabur production H601 UI readback, or the 2026-07-01 Gallery loading fallback / mass-market route QA slice. However, G617 same-run all-10 fresh generation remains incomplete: the old Runway path hit connected workspace/model unavailability (`workspace_limit`) after a successful minimal probe, and the Gemini standard path now reaches Google Generative Language with a valid AI Studio key but returns `RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED` because Nano Banana / Nano Banana 2 / Nano Banana Lite / Nano Banana Pro image-generation free-tier quota is `limit: 0` for this project; Imagen 4 also requires a paid plan. The older `mcp-remote` localhost consent path remains invalid historical evidence, not current restart path. Next generation acceptance should use Gemini after eligible image-generation quota is available for the project behind the active key, then rerun marker-scoped live generation, DB/Storage/Gallery/Jobs/Canvas readback, visual scorecard, and cleanup; Runway can remain an explicit fallback only if the approved existing Runway MCP client can sustain tasks. G619 still needs real consented beta sessions. H601 local source guard covers UI/helper generation and edit paths plus shared server-side generation Edge Function entrypoints, `npm run verify:h601-legal-safety` passes, and Zeabur production H601 rights/caveat/checkbox readback passes; H601 final legal/operator decisions remain human-needed. The custom domain `https://heavy-chain.com` is currently unreachable with `ERR_CONNECTION_REFUSED`; use `https://heavy-chain.zeabur.app` for verified production QA until DNS/hosting is fixed. Full 10M public-launch completion remains unproven until live Gemini-or-approved-Runway all-feature generation proof, G619, H601 final human decisions, H602 completion, public entrypoint/domain, and final release gates are closed.
risk_gate: Billing, purchase, payment, checkout, identity verification, security prompts, secret entry, external public publishing, and final legal-policy approval still require explicit human approval. Non-billing production UAT may click generation submit only after the upload-rights confirmation gate, create marker-scoped jobs/images/usage rows, verify DB/Storage/UI readback, and clean up only those marker-scoped test artifacts. Future storage/auth/RLS/deploy/external-data changes require fresh proof.
maturity_candidate: release_prep
record_replay_computer_use: Record & Replay / Computer Use may be used for stable production readback, visual parity checks, Runway bridge status inspection, and Codex app/desktop GUI steps when Playwright/API/file proof is insufficient. They must not replace root `STATE.md`, deployment/readback proof, DB/readback artifacts, `verify:*` commands, or cleanup proof. Deployment, secrets, storage/auth/RLS, external service settings, billing, purchase, payment, and checkout still require explicit approval or a fresh workflow contract.

lightchain_direct_tool_screens_20260701: G661 makes Lightchain tool cards open their dedicated `/lightchain/:toolId` screens directly rather than relying on an in-page side detail prompt. Local proof `output/playwright/local-g661-lightchain-direct-tool-links-20260701-r2/SUMMARY.json` passed with `ok=true`, `failed=[]`, cleanup closed, and desktop/mobile direct-detail route assertions. Production proof is now adopted from the G662 broad production QA `output/playwright/prod-post-g662-gemini-provider-default-20260701-r3/SUMMARY.json`, which passed with `ok=true`, `failed=[]`, zero console/page/request failures, cleanup closed, `lightchain_tool_cards_open_detail_routes`, `mobile_lightchain_tool_cards_open_detail_routes`, and `mobile_dashboard_lightchain_cards_open_detail_routes`. This improves Lightchain feature-screen usability but does not close G617/G619/H601/H602 or the custom domain blocker.
gemini_provider_default_groundwork_20260701: G662 switches the standard source/config/docs path from Runway worker to Gemini for normal image generation. `generate-image` now accepts `generationProvider`, defaults to Gemini, calls `_shared/geminiImage.ts`, records provider metadata, returns image arrays, and only requires Runway MCP approval for explicit Runway/local-worker paths. `_shared/geminiImage.ts` uses server-side `GEMINI_API_KEY`, supports `GEMINI_IMAGE_MODEL`, parses inline image responses, and fails closed as `gemini_api_key_missing` / `gemini_image_request_failed` / `gemini_image_empty_response` without exposing secrets. Frontend `/generate` defaults to `VITE_GENERATION_PROVIDER=gemini`, shows `Geminiで生成`, and sends `generationProvider: gemini`; docs/env examples and static gates now treat Gemini as the standard runtime provider while keeping Runway as explicit fallback/legacy. Proof passed: `npm run verify:gemini-provider`, `npm run smoke:edge`, `npm run typecheck -- --pretty false`, `npm run lint -- --max-warnings=0`, `npm run build`, `deno check supabase/functions/_shared/geminiImage.ts supabase/functions/generate-image/index.ts`, `git diff --check`, and local mass-market QA `output/playwright/local-g662-gemini-provider-default-20260701-r1/SUMMARY.json` with `ok=true`, `failed=[]`, 17 desktop routes, 9 mobile routes, zero console/page/request failures, cleanup closed, and screenshot inspection of desktop/mobile `Geminiで生成`. Production readback after GitHub push passed: Zeabur serves `assets/index.CYduUJuB.js`, Generate chunk contains `Geminiで生成`, and production mass-market QA `output/playwright/prod-post-g662-gemini-provider-default-20260701-r3/SUMMARY.json` has `ok=true`, `failed=[]`, 17 desktop routes, 9 mobile routes, zero console/page/request failures, cleanup closed, and desktop/mobile Generate buttons show `Geminiで生成`; r1/r2 are not adopted because r1 had browser close timeout and r2 saw transient Canvas 503. API key was not provided in this session, so live Gemini generation, all-10 scorecard, and any G617 replacement proof remain unproven.
release_completion_gate_g663_gemini_wiring_20260701: G663 wires G662 into the durable release/completion proof layer. `scripts/verify-release-gate-unified.mjs` now reads `output/playwright/prod-post-g662-gemini-provider-default-20260701-r3/SUMMARY.json` as `production mass-market QA current` and requires desktop/mobile Generate assertion details to include `Geminiで生成`. `scripts/verify-10m-completion-audit.mjs` now uses `g662_current_production_mass_market_qa`, accepts `accepted-*` statuses such as `accepted-prod-ui`, and avoids false conflicts when summary and table statuses are both accepted variants. Checks passed: `node --check scripts/verify-release-gate-unified.mjs`, `node --check scripts/verify-10m-completion-audit.mjs`, `git diff --check`. Clean release gate `output/playwright/release-gate-g663-current-clean-20260701-r1` reads G662 production QA as passed, confirms all 23 commands passed, and fails only on `production public domain readback` and `production H602 billing completion readback`. Completion audit `output/playwright/10m-completion-audit-20260701-g663-r2/summary.json` removes the false G621-G663 status blockers and preserves only real blockers for G617, G619, H601/H602, public domain, H602 completion, G619 verifier, and release gate.
production_monitor_g664_refresh_20260701: G664 refreshes production monitor after the G662/G663 Gemini UI release-gate wiring. `npm run monitor:production -- --out output/playwright/production-monitor-post-g663-20260701-r1` passed with `ok=true`, blockers `0`, warnings `1`, generation failure rate `0`, stale active jobs `0`, storage errors `0`, and `uiOk=true`; the warning is only `local_worker_inbox_stale_files` for 4 local Runway MCP audit files. `scripts/verify-release-gate-unified.mjs` now reads this monitor proof. Clean release gate `output/playwright/release-gate-g664-current-clean-20260701-r2` reads the latest monitor and G662 production QA as passed, all 23 commands pass, and fails only on `production public domain readback` and `production H602 billing completion readback`. Completion audit `output/playwright/10m-completion-audit-20260701-g664-r1/summary.json` preserves only real blockers for G617, G619, H601/H602, public domain, H602 completion, G619 verifier, and release gate.
gemini_live_path_g665_20260701: G665 turns Gemini from UI/default wiring into an exercised production live path. `scripts/hc-10m-real-generation-qa.mjs` now has `invoke-gemini` / `npm run qa:g617-gemini-live`, reusing the G617 all-feature manifest/readback/cleanup shape with authenticated production `generate-image` invokes, `generationProvider=gemini`, rights confirmation, source metadata, and Lightchain compatibility metadata. `scripts/verify-10m-completion-audit.mjs` can accept completed Gemini DB/Storage artifacts for G617 instead of requiring Runway-only `workerImageIds`. Production DB migration `20260701143000_roll_free_subscription_period_for_usage_reservation.sql` was applied with `supabase db push`, rolling active free-plan periods across month boundaries without checkout/payment/credential changes. `generate-image` was deployed twice: first to load Gemini, then again after fixing the H601 legal-safety false positive where short protected brand term `lv` matched inside normal words like `silver`. Current live proof `output/playwright/g617-gemini-live-generation-20260701-r5/run-manifest.json` reached production Gemini and failed exactly at `API_KEY_INVALID`; `readback-after-invalid-api-key.json` shows one failed job and zero images/storage, `cleanup-after-invalid-api-key.json` and `readback-after-cleanup-invalid-api-key.json` prove zero residual jobs/images. Clean release gate `output/playwright/release-gate-g665-current-clean-20260701-r1` fails only on public domain and H602 completion. Completion audit `output/playwright/10m-completion-audit-20260701-g665-r2/summary.json` keeps only the known G617/G619/H601/H602/public-domain/H602-completion blockers. Checks passed: `npm run verify:h601-legal-safety`, `npm run verify:h602-billing`, `npm run verify:gemini-provider -- --out output/playwright/g665-gemini-live-path-hardening/summary.json`, `npm run verify:release-gate -- --out output/playwright/release-gate-g665-current-clean-20260701-r1`, `npm run verify:10m-completion -- --out output/playwright/10m-completion-audit-20260701-g665-r2 --allow-incomplete`, `npm run typecheck -- --pretty false`, `npm run lint -- --max-warnings=0`, `npm run build`, `deno check supabase/functions/_shared/legalSafety.ts supabase/functions/generate-image/index.ts`, and `git diff --check`. Next step after receiving a valid Gemini API key: set/replace only the server-side Supabase Function secret `GEMINI_API_KEY`, redeploy/confirm `generate-image`, rerun `npm run qa:g617-gemini-live -- --outDir <new-g617-dir> --runId <new-run-id>` for all 10 features, then run readback, visual scorecard, cleanup, release gate, and 10M completion audit.
production_monitor_g666_refresh_20260701: G666 refreshes production monitor after the G665 Gemini live-path probe and wires release gate to the latest monitor proof. `npm run monitor:production -- --out output/playwright/production-monitor-post-g665-20260701-r1` passed with `ok=true`, blockers `0`, generation failure rate `0`, stale active jobs `0`, storage errors `0`, and `uiOk=true`. route_aware_loading_g667_20260701: G667 fixes the post-G666 logged-in route loading regression where Dashboard, Jobs, or Gallery could be captured as spinner-only during auth or lazy chunk waits. `src/App.tsx` now uses route-aware loading copy for Dashboard, Generate, Lightchain, Jobs, Gallery, Canvas, History, Credits, Brand Settings, and the apparel workspaces, with safe reload/login recovery during protected auth waits. `scripts/verify-mass-market-qa.mjs` now waits for the real route body and avoids accepting loading fallback copy as route proof. Release/completion gates now read the G667 production QA proof and G667 monitor. This does not close valid Gemini key, G617, G619, H601/H602, or public-domain blockers.
dashboard_recent_preview_g668_20260702: G668 fixes the Dashboard `最近の生成` trust regression without using Gemini API. `DashboardPage` now renders recent image cards only when an image has a usable URL and has not failed to load; unresolved or failed preview items move into a calm recovery panel with Gallery and Jobs actions instead of showing broken gray `画像なし` cards. `scripts/verify-mass-market-qa.mjs` now requires `dashboard_recent_images_no_broken_placeholders` on Dashboard. gemini_live_secret_g669_20260702: G669 sets the active AI Studio Gemini key only as server-side Supabase Function secret `GEMINI_API_KEY`; no key was written to source files. Secret readback was name-only and confirmed update time `2026-07-01T16:56:13.365Z`; no `GEMINI_IMAGE_MODEL` secret override exists, so production uses the source default `gemini-2.5-flash-image` (Nano Banana). A direct text probe confirmed the key is valid. Direct image probes confirmed `gemini-2.5-flash-image`, `gemini-3.1-flash-lite-image`, `gemini-3.1-flash-image`, `gemini-3.1-flash-image-preview`, `gemini-3-pro-image`, `gemini-3-pro-image-preview`, and `nano-banana-pro-preview` all fail for this project with `RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED` / free-tier image quota `limit: 0`; Imagen 4 Fast/Standard/Ultra fail as paid-plan-required. Production proof `output/playwright/g669-gemini-validkey-probe-20260701T165624Z/run-manifest.json` shows the `campaign-image` marker job reached Gemini image generation and failed at the same quota layer; `readback.json`, `cleanup.json`, and `readback-after-cleanup.log` prove no residual jobs/images after cleanup. G617 all-10 generation, scorecard, and release completion are still not accepted.

openai_mini_heavy_chain_g677_20260702: OpenAI is now proven through the real Heavy Chain production server path using the low-cost `gpt-image-1-mini` model. `OPENAI_IMAGE_API_KEY` and `OPENAI_IMAGE_MODEL` were set only as Supabase Function secrets, `generate-image` was deployed, a one-image `campaign-image` marker probe passed with 1 completed job / 1 image / 1 Storage object / signed URL OK, and cleanup returned 0 residual jobs/images. ai_fitting_canvas_direct_ux_g689_20260703: G689 implements the follow-up UX fixes from the user's screen recordings/screenshots. Canvas was moved toward the Heavy Chain dark direction and now exposes image-first actions (`背景を消す`, `色を変える`, `高解像度`, `派生させる`, `指示で編集`, `新しく生成`); prompt edit calls the real `edit-image` API and edit failures show toast errors. Canvas model-matrix only sends `modelReferenceImageUrl` when a garment/product reference image is also present, so product-description-only generation is not blocked or routed into a model-reference API failure. Edit modal prompt/colorize/variations paths now apply the same local legal-safety text guard and rights/brand gates before API calls. Lightchain feature cards now route directly to their actual feature routes instead of `/lightchain/:toolId` intermediate pages; Lab copy/action text clarifies it as `ウェアデザインラボ`; Brand Settings removed the top `生成前に整えること` and `Runway MCP接続` blocks. Checks passed: `npm run typecheck -- --pretty false`, `npm run lint -- --max-warnings=0`, `npm run build`, `git diff --check`, `deno check supabase/functions/model-matrix/index.ts`, static readbacks `output/playwright/g689-static-ui-followup-readback-r2/summary.json` through `r5/summary.json`, and final read-only Codex review returned `重大/中程度の問題なし`. Visible local Playwright verification was attempted on preview `http://127.0.0.1:4191` but blocked by `local_visible_auth_state_redirected_to_login`; screenshot/debug artifacts are under `output/playwright/g689-visible-ui-followup-debug/`. This is local/internal-beta implementation proof only; production deploy/readback, G617 strict same-run, G619 real beta evidence, H601/H602 decisions, billing, checkout, payment, and public-launch readiness remain open.

ai_fitting_white_background_fallback_g692_20260703: Responded to the live user report that `/fitting` could not cut out a white shirt on a white/light background because `isnet-general-use.onnx` returned HTTP 404, leaving AI generation blocked. Root cause remains production model hosting/configuration, but the fitting UI now has a quality-gated browser fallback for flat white/background product photos when the ONNX model fails to load. The fallback only accepts edge-connected background-flood cutouts with real transparency and rejects full-frame/geometric fallback (`boundsRatio > 0.92` or non-flood engine), so weak cutouts do not unlock generation. White-garment detection was tightened so bright garments are less likely to be mistaken for bright backgrounds. Visible Playwright CLI proof on local preview `http://127.0.0.1:4193/fitting#fitting-material-workbench` forced `/models/isnet-general-use.onnx` to HTTP 404, uploaded `output/playwright/g684-live-fitting-operation-r1/shirt-input-from-user-screenshot.png`, reached `高精度AI切り抜き済みです`, checked rights, and confirmed `AI生成` became enabled without clicking generation. Checks passed: `npm run typecheck -- --pretty false`, `npm run lint -- --max-warnings=0`, `npm run build`, `npm run verify:rembg-model-deploy`, `npm run verify:internal-ux -- --out output/playwright/g692-white-background-cutout-internal-ux-r2`, and `git diff --check`. Read-only Codex review found one fallback-quality gap and it was fixed by requiring flood-cutout provenance; final Codex review re-ran typecheck/lint/build/diff/rembg/internal-ux and found no high/medium issue. This is a local implementation/proof slice; production deployment/readback still needs commit/push/deploy verification, and a CORS-enabled `VITE_REMBG_MODEL_BASE_URL` remains the preferred true high-precision model-hosting fix.

ai_fitting_white_background_fallback_g692_followup_20260703: Production readback after the first G692 deploy still failed because `@bunnio/rembg-web` deferred the `isnet-general-use.onnx` fetch until `remove()` / session prediction, so the earlier `newSession()` catch did not catch the real 404. The high-precision cutout path now also catches model download failures during `remove()`, resets the cached session, and falls back to the bounded white-background cutout only for quality-gated flood-cutout results. The white-shirt/screenshot-edge case was also tightened: background estimation now samples bright neutral border medians so black screenshot/browser edges do not poison the background color, bright-background flood fill treats dark edge-connected bands as background, and near-background feathering can reach alpha 0 so transparent halos do not keep the crop full-frame. Direct dev readback on `output/playwright/g684-live-fitting-operation-r1/shirt-input-from-user-screenshot.png` returned `engine=browser-canvas-background-flood-cutout-v2`, `boundsRatio=0.9154`, `dataUrlBytes=106427`, which passes the fallback gate. Checks passed before production deploy: `npm run typecheck -- --pretty false`, `npm run lint -- --max-warnings=0`, `npm run build` with expected asset `assets/index.BTtAiT0H.js`, `npm run verify:rembg-model-deploy`, `npm run verify:internal-ux -- --out output/playwright/g692-white-shirt-edge-fallback-internal-ux-r1`, `git diff --check`, and read-only Codex review with `npm run verify:internal-ux -- --out output/playwright/g692-codex-review-internal-ux-r1`; the review found no high/medium issue. Production deploy/readback is next and not yet claimed in this entry.

ai_fitting_model_matrix_generation_g693_20260703: Fixed the user-reported blocker where `/fitting` could cut out a garment but `AI生成` failed with `Edge Function returned a non-2xx status code`. Root cause: `supabase/functions/model-matrix` was still hard-wired to the old Runway MCP approval/generation path while the production generation stack had moved to OpenAI image generation; therefore cutout success did not guarantee fitting generation. `model-matrix` now uses OpenAI image edit when garment/model references are present, uses OpenAI text image generation only when no references are supplied, records provider metadata as `openai`, reserves usage per requested matrix cell, and no longer requires Runway MCP approval for this AI fitting path. `src/lib/imageApi.ts` now surfaces the Edge Function JSON error body instead of the generic Supabase SDK message. Production deploy/readback succeeded on `https://heavy-chain.zeabur.app`: focused 1x1 authenticated smoke created a completed `model-matrix` job, inserted one `generated_images` row with `model_used=gpt-image-1-mini`, downloaded the generated Storage object (`1877165` bytes), then cleaned up the test image row, job row, and Storage file. Proof: `output/playwright/g693-fitting-model-matrix-openai-r1/summary.json`. Earlier failed proof `output/playwright/g693-fitting-model-matrix-openai-r1/summary-r1-failed-input-fidelity.json` records the OpenAI edit-model compatibility blocker that was fixed by removing unsupported `input_fidelity` for `gpt-image-1-mini`. Checks passed: `npm run typecheck -- --pretty false`, `deno check supabase/functions/_shared/openaiImage.ts supabase/functions/model-matrix/index.ts`, `git diff --check`, `npm run lint -- --max-warnings=0`, `npm run build`, and `npm run verify:h601-legal-safety`. Reference image fetch is now guarded to image data URLs or same Supabase project http(s) URLs only, rejects redirects/local/private hosts, requires allowed image MIME types, and caps streamed reference bodies at 8MiB. Test generation only was performed and cleaned up; no billing, checkout, payment, identity verification, CAPTCHA/OTP, external publishing, or legal finalization was performed. Remaining launch blockers stay separate: G617 strict same-run proof, G619 real beta evidence, H601/H602 human decisions, and public-launch readiness.

ai_fitting_reference_normalization_g700_20260704: Fixed the current AI fitting cutout/generation reliability issues without billing, checkout, payment, identity verification, CAPTCHA/OTP, external publishing, or quota bypass. `/fitting` now normalizes garment/model data URLs to bounded JPEG with a light gray background before sending them to `model-matrix`, so transparent PNG/special canvas output is not passed directly to OpenAI image edit. The high-precision cutout path now treats rembg model download/session/backend failures and timeouts as recoverable and falls back to the quality-gated white-background garment cutout; Playwright/WebGL-unavailable verification uses that fallback instead of hanging at `AI切り抜き中`. The `AI生成` and `再試行` handlers now use a synchronous click lock to prevent duplicate paid/usage-reserved generation requests before React disabled state renders. ai_fitting_model_matrix_prod_unblocked_g705_20260704: Fixed the production AI fitting generation failure without billing, checkout, payment, identity verification, CAPTCHA/OTP, external publishing, destructive cleanup, or quota bypass. Root cause was the `/fitting` / model-matrix default requesting 4 patterns (`slim,regular` x `20s,30s`) against the current 3-unit/minute user rate limit, causing `User usage rate limit exceeded`. The app now defaults Fitting/Generate/Canvas model-matrix entry points to one pattern (`regular` x `20s`), blocks >3 patterns in UI, and the `model-matrix` Edge Function rejects >3 requested units before usage reservation. Commit `23dca92` was pushed to `origin/main`; `model-matrix` Edge Function was deployed to Supabase project `ghwjymozrwmcrpjqvbmo`; production served expected asset `assets/index.egBvsFYj.js`. The visible Playwright CLI verifier was hardened to wait for the submitted response only, load `.env.production.local`, ignore placeholder Supabase URL/key values, read back DB/Storage via REST, download generated PNGs, and future submit-mode acceptance now requires explicit visual review tied to the downloaded artifact path or SHA. Existing visible production E2E proof `output/playwright/g705-visible-fitting-prod-submit-post-deploy-r8-accepted/summary.json` is the technical/UI proof: `technicalOk=true`, `responseStatus=200`, `jobStatus=completed`, one DB generated image, Storage PNG download `downloadedBytes=2402422`, plus video/screenshots. The final visual acceptance proof is separate: downloaded output `output/playwright/g705-visible-fitting-prod-submit-post-deploy-r8-accepted/downloaded-6f737093-c517-4f54-8eed-6fe73da3805b.png` has SHA `05a5b3462056b5d600cb1ba608bdbd8de3b417c0a99830dce0a94216c6078163`, and `output/playwright/g705-visible-fitting-prod-visual-review-r1/visual-review.json` binds the accepted visual review to that exact SHA/downloaded artifact. Visual review result: the white shirt is naturally worn, with no major collar/sleeve/front-placket/hem breakage.
